AlertTriangle, BookOpen, Copy, Ellipsis, FileText, LoaderCircle, RefreshCw, WrapText } from \"lucide-react\";\n     6\timport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\n     7\timport {\n     8\t  DropdownMenu,\n     9\t  DropdownMenuCheckboxItem,\n    10\t  DropdownMenuContent,\n    11\t  DropdownMenuItem,\n    12\t  DropdownMenuSeparator,\n    13\t  DropdownMenuTrigger,\n    14\t} from \"@/components/ui/dropdown-menu\";\n    15\timport { MarkdownContent } from \"@/components/MarkdownContent\";\n    16\timport { fileViewerPanelManager } from \"@/components/component-state-managers\";\n    17\timport { formatBytes } from \"@/lib/chat-attachments\";\n    18\timport { t, useI18nSnapshot } from \"@/lib/i18n\";\n    19\timport { buildProjectFileFullPath } from \"@/lib/project-file-links\";\n    20\timport { detectSyntaxLanguage, highlightCodeLine } from \"@/lib/syntax-highlighting\";\n    21\timport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\";\n    22\timport { useManagerSnapshot } from \"@/lib/use-manager-snapshot\";\n    23\timport type { ProjectFileContentResponse } from \"@/interface/home/types\";\n    24\timport { useRuntimeAPIs } from \"@/runtime-api/provider\";\n    25\t\n    26\texport function FileViewerPanel({\n    27\t  root,\n    28\t  relativePath,\n    29\t  line,\n    30\t  className,\n    31\t}: {\n    32\t  root: string;\n    33\t  relativePath: string;\n    34\t  line?: number;\n    35\t  className?: string;\n    36\t}) {\n    37\t  const targetLineRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n    38\t  const { wordWrap, renderMarkdown } = useManagerSnapshot(fileViewerPanelManager);\n    39\t  const isMarkdown = /\\.(md|mdx|markdown)$/i.test(relativePath);\n    40\t  const renderAsMarkdown = isMarkdown && renderMarkdown;\n    41\t  useI18nSnapshot();\n    42\t  const runtimeApis = useRuntimeAPIs();\n    43\t  const fileQuery = useQuery<ProjectFileContentResponse>({\n    44\t    queryKey: [\"project-file\", root, relativePath],\n    45\t    queryFn: () => runtimeApis.files.list({\n    46\t      root,\n    47\t      file: relativePath,\n    48\t    }) as Promise<ProjectFileContentResponse>,\n    49\t    enabled: Boolean(root && relativePath),\n    50\t    staleTime: 30_000,\n    51\t  });\n    52\t\n    53\t  useEffect(() => {\n    54\t    targetLineRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ block: \"center\" });\n    55\t  }, [fileQuery.data?.content, line]);\n    56\t\n    57\t  const content = fileQuery.data?.content ?? \"\";\n    58\t  const lines = useMemo(() => (\n    59\t    content.length > 0 ? content.split(\"\\n\") : [\"\"]\n    60\t  ), [content]);\n    61\t  const syntaxLanguage = useMemo(() => detectSyntaxLanguage(relativePath), [relativePath]);\n    62\t  const highlightedLines = useMemo(\n    63\t    () => lines.map((text) => highlightCodeLine(text, syntaxLanguage)),\n    64\t    [lines, syntaxLanguage],\n    65\t  );\n    66\t\n    67\t  return (\n    68\t    <div className={cn(\"flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col bg-background\", className)}>\n    69\t      <div className=\"shrink-0 border-b border-border/60 px-3 py-2.5\">\n    70\t        <div className=\"flex min-w-0 items-start gap-2\">\n    71\t          <div className=\"flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-2\">\n    72\t            <FileText className=\"h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground\" />\n    73\t            <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n    74\t              <div className=\"truncate font-mono text-xs font-semibold text-foreground\" title={relativePath}>\n    75\t                {relativePath}\n    76\t              </div>\n    77\t              <div className=\"truncate font-mono text-[10px] text-muted-foreground\" title={root}>\n    78\t                {root}\n    79\t              </div>\n    80\t            </div>\n    81\t          </div>\n    82\t          <DropdownMenu>\n    83\t            <DropdownMenuTrigger\n    84\t              render={<Button variant=\"ghost\" size=\"icon\" className=\"h-7 w-7 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground\" />}\n    85\t              aria-label={t(\"fileViewer.menu.label\")}\n    86\t              title={t(\"fileViewer.menu.label\")}\n    87\t            >\n    88\t              <Ellipsis className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n    89\t            </DropdownMenuTrigger>\n    90\t            <DropdownMenuContent align=\"end\" className=\"min-w-40\">\n    91\t              {isMarkdown ? (\n    92\t                <DropdownMenuCheckboxItem\n    93\t                  checked={renderMarkdown}\n    94\t                  onCheckedChange={() => fileViewerPanelManager.toggleRenderMarkdown()}\n    95\t                >\n    96\t                  <BookOpen className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n    97\t                  <span>{t(\"fileViewer.menu.renderMarkdown\")}</span>\n    98\t                </DropdownMenuCheckboxItem>\n    99\t              ) : null}\n   100\t              <DropdownMenuCheckboxItem\n   101\t                checked={wordWrap}\n   102\t                onCheckedChange={() => fileViewerPanelManager.toggleWordWrap()}\n   103\t              >\n   104\t                <WrapText className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n   105\t                <span>{t(\"fileViewer.menu.wordWrap\")}</span>\n   106\t              </DropdownMenuCheckboxItem>\n   107\t              <DropdownMenuSeparator />\n   108\t              <DropdownMenuItem\n   109\t                onClick={() => {\n   110\t                  void navigator.clipboard.writeText(buildProjectFileFullPath({ root, relativePath }));\n   111\t                }}\n   112\t              >\n   113\t                <Copy className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n   114\t                <span>{t(\"fileViewer.menu.copyFullPath\")}</span>\n   115\t              </DropdownMenuItem>\n   116\t              <DropdownMenuItem\n   117\t                onClick={() => {\n   118\t                  void fileQuery.refetch();\n   119\t                }}\n   120\t              >\n   121\t                <RefreshCw className={cn(\"h-4 w-4\", fileQuery.isFetching && \"animate-spin\")} />\n   122\t                <span>{t(\"fileViewer.menu.refresh\")}</span>\n   123\t              </DropdownMenuItem>\n   124\t            </DropdownMenuContent>\n   125\t          </DropdownMenu>\n   126\t        </div>\n   127\t        {fileQuery.data ? (\n   128\t          <div className=\"mt-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground\">\n   129\t            <span>{formatBytes(fileQuery.data.size)}</span>\n   130\t            {line ? <span>{t(\"fileViewer.metadata.line\", { line })}</span> : null}\n   131\t            {syntaxLanguage ? <span className=\"uppercase\">{syntaxLanguage}</span> : null}\n   132\t            {fileQuery.data.truncated ? (\n   133\t              <span className=\"rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-medium text-amber-700\">\n   134\t                {t(\"fileViewer.metadata.truncated\")}\n   135\t              </span>\n   136\t            ) : null}\n   137\t          </div>\n   138\t        ) : null}\n   139\t      </div>\n   140\t\n   141\t      {fileQuery.isLoading ? (\n   142\t        <div className=\"flex min-h-0 flex-1 items-center justify-center text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n   143\t          <LoaderCircle className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin\" />\n   144\t          {t(\"fileViewer.loading\")}\n   145\t        </div>\n   146\t      ) : fileQuery.error ? (\n   147\t        <div className=\"m-3 rounded-lg border border-destructive/25 bg-destructive/5 p-3 text-sm text-destructive\">\n   148\t          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold\">\n   149\t            <AlertTriangle className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n   150\t            {t(\"fileViewer.errorTitle\")}\n   151\t          </div>\n   152\t          <p className=\"mt-1 break-words text-xs leading-5\">\n   153\t            {fileQuery.error instanceof Error ? fileQuery.error.message : String(fileQuery.error)}\n   154\t          </p>\n   155\t        </div>\n   156\t      ) : renderAsMarkdown ? (\n   157\t        <div className=\"omni-conversation-text-scale min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto bg-background [scrollbar-width:thin]\">\n   158\t          <MarkdownContent content={content} className=\"px-4 py-3\" projectRoot={root} />\n   159\t        </div>\n   160\t      ) : (\n   161\t        <div className=\"omni-conversation-text-scale min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto bg-muted/15 [scrollbar-width:thin]\">\n   162\t          <pre className={cn(\"syntax-highlight py-3 font-mono text-[length:var(--omni-conversation-font-size)] leading-[var(--omni-conversation-line-height)]\", wordWrap ? \"min-w-0\" : \"min-w-max\")}>\n   163\t            {lines.map((text, index) => {\n   164\t              const lineNumber = index + 1;\n   165\t              const highlighted = lineNumber === line;\n   166\t              return (\n   167\t                <div\n   168\t                  key={lineNumber}\n   169\t                  ref={highlighted ? targetLineRef : undefined}\n   170\t                  className={cn(\n   171\t                    wordWrap ? \"grid grid-cols-[4rem_minmax(0,1fr)] px-3\" : \"grid min-w-max grid-cols-[4rem_max-content] px-3\",\n   172\t                    highlighted && \"bg-primary/10 text-primary\",\n   173\t                  )}\n   174\t                >\n   175\t                  <span className=\"select-none pr-4 text-right text-muted-foreground/70\">{lineNumber}</span>\n   176\t                  <code\n   177\t                    className={cn(\n   178\t                      \"pr-4 text-foreground\",\n   179\t                      wordWrap ? \"whitespace-pre-wrap break-words\" : \"whitespace-pre\",\n   180\t                    )}\n   181\t                    dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: highlightedLines[index] ?? \" \" }}\n   182\t                  />\n   183\t                </div>\n   184\t              );\n   185\t            })}\n   186\t          </pre>\n   187\t        </div>\n   188\t      )}\n   189\t    </div>\n   190\t  );\n   191\t}\n\nFILES ROUTE\n     1\timport fs from \"fs\";\n     2\timport path from \"path\";\n     3\t\n     4\tconst IGNORED_DIRECTORIES = new Set([\n     5\t  \".git\",\n     6\t  \".hg\",\n     7\t  \".svn\",\n     8\t  \".next\",\n     9\t  \".turbo\",\n    10\t  \".cache\",\n    11\t  \"node_modules\",\n    12\t  \"__pycache__\",\n    13\t  \"dist\",\n    14\t  \"build\",\n    15\t  \"coverage\",\n    16\t  \"target\",\n    17\t  \"out\",\n    18\t]);\n    19\t\n    20\tconst DEFAULT_TEXT_FILE_MAX_BYTES = 512 * 1024;\n    21\t\n    22\texport type ProjectFileContent = {\n    23\t  root: string;\n    24\t  path: string;\n    25\t  content: string;\n    26\t  size: number;\n    27\t  truncated: boolean;\n    28\t};\n    29\t\n    30\texport type ReadProjectTextFileOptions = {\n    31\t  maxBytes?: number;\n    32\t};\n    33\t\n    34\texport function isPathInside(root: string, candidate: string) {\n    35\t  const relative = path.relative(root, candidate);\n    36\t  return relative === \"\" || (!relative.startsWith(\"..\") && !path.isAbsolute(relative));\n    37\t}\n    38\t\n    39\tfunction walkFiles(root: string, currentDir: string, files: string[]) {\n    40\t  const entries = fs.readdirSync(currentDir, { withFileTypes: true });\n    41\t\n    42\t  for (const entry of entries) {\n    43\t    if (entry.name.startsWith(\".\") && entry.name !== \".env\") {\n    44\t      continue;\n    45\t    }\n    46\t\n    47\t    const absolutePath = path.join(currentDir, entry.name);\n    48\t    if (entry.isDirectory()) {\n    49\t      if (IGNORED_DIRECTORIES.has(entry.name)) {\n    50\t        continue;\n    51\t      }\n    52\t      walkFiles(root, absolutePath, files);\n    53\t      continue;\n    54\t    }\n    55\t\n    56\t    if (!entry.isFile()) {\n    57\t      continue;\n    58\t    }\n    59\t\n    60\t    files.push(path.relative(root, absolutePath));\n    61\t  }\n    62\t}\n    63\t\n    64\texport function listProjectFiles(root: string) {\n    65\t  const resolvedRoot = path.resolve(root);\n    66\t  const files: string[] = [];\n    67\t  walkFiles(resolvedRoot, resolvedRoot, files);\n    68\t  return files.sort((left, right) => left.localeCompare(right));\n    69\t}\n    70\t\n    71\tfunction looksBinary(buffer: Buffer) {\n    72\t  if (buffer.includes(0)) {\n    73\t    return true;\n    74\t  }\n    75\t\n    76\t  const text = buffer.toString(\"utf8\");\n    77\t  if (!text.includes(\"\\uFFFD\")) {\n    78\t    return false;\n    79\t  }\n    80\t\n    81\t  const replacementCount = Array.from(text).filter((char) => char === \"\\uFFFD\").length;\n    82\t  return replacementCount > Math.max(2, text.length * 0.01);\n    83\t}\n    84\t\n    85\texport function readProjectTextFile(\n    86\t  root: string,\n    87\t  relativePath: string,\n    88\t  options: ReadProjectTextFileOptions = {},\n    89\t): ProjectFileContent {\n    90\t  const resolvedRoot = path.resolve(root);\n    91\t  const normalizedRelativePath = relativePath.replace(/\\\\/g, path.sep);\n    92\t  const absolutePath = path.resolve(resolvedRoot, normalizedRelativePath);\n    93\t  if (!isPathInside(resolvedRoot, absolutePath)) {\n    94\t    throw new Error(\"File path is outside the project root.\");\n    95\t  }\n    96\t\n    97\t  const stat = fs.statSync(absolutePath);\n    98\t  if (!stat.isFile()) {\n    99\t    throw new Error(\"Project path is not a file.\");\n   100\t  }\n   101\t\n   102\t  const maxBytes = Math.max(1, options.maxBytes ?? DEFAULT_TEXT_FILE_MAX_BYTES);\n   103\t  const file = fs.openSync(absolutePath, \"r\");\n   104\t  try {\n   105\t    const readLength = Math.min(stat.size, maxBytes + 1);\n   106\t    const buffer = Buffer.alloc(readLength);\n   107\t    const bytesRead = fs.readSync(file, buffer, 0, readLength, 0);\n   108\t    const readBuffer = buffer.subarray(0, bytesRead);\n   109\t    if (looksBinary(readBuffer)) {\n   110\t      throw new Error(\"Project file appears to be binary and cannot be displayed.\");\n   111\t    }\n   112\t\n   113\t    const truncated = stat.size > maxBytes;\n   114\t    const contentBuffer = truncated ? readBuffer.subarray(0, maxBytes) : readBuffer;\n   115\t    return {\n   116\t      root: resolvedRoot,\n   117\t      path: path.relative(resolvedRoot, absolutePath).replace(/\\\\/g, \"/\"),\n   118\t      content: contentBuffer.toString(\"utf8\"),\n   119\t      size: stat.size,\n   120\t      truncated,\n   121\t    };\n   122\t  } finally {\n   123\t    fs.closeSync(file);\n   124\t  }\n   125\t}\n\nMARKDOWN\n     1\timport React from \"react\";\n     2\timport { ProjectFileContextMenu } from \"@/components/ProjectFileContextMenu\";\n     3\timport { parseProjectFileReference, type ProjectFileReference } from \"@/lib/project-file-links\";\n     4\timport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\";\n     5\t\n     6\tfunction isSafeHref(href: string) {\n     7\t  return /^(https?:\\/\\/|mailto:|\\/|#)/.test(href);\n     8\t}\n     9\t\n    10\ttype Alignment = \"left\" | \"center\" | \"right\" | null;\n    11\t\n    12\tfunction isTableDelimiter(line: string): boolean {\n    13\t  const trimmed = line.trim();\n    14\t  if (!trimmed.includes(\"|\")) return false;\n    15\t  let content = trimmed;\n    16\t  if (content.startsWith(\"|\")) content = content.slice(1);\n    17\t  if (content.endsWith(\"|\")) content = content.slice(0, -1);\n    18\t\n    19\t  const columns = content.split(\"|\");\n    20\t  if (columns.length === 0) return false;\n    21\t\n    22\t  for (const col of columns) {\n    23\t    const colTrim = col.trim();\n    24\t    if (!/^[:-]+$/.test(colTrim) || !colTrim.includes(\"-\")) {\n    25\t      return false;\n    26\t    }\n    27\t  }\n    28\t  return true;\n    29\t}\n    30\t\n    31\tfunction splitTableRow(line: string): string[] {\n    32\t  let content = line.trim();\n    33\t  if (content.startsWith(\"|\")) content = content.slice(1);\n    34\t  if (content.endsWith(\"|\")) content = content.slice(0, -1);\n    35\t  return content.split(/(?<!\\\\)\\|/).map((col) => col.replace(/\\\\\\|/g, \"|\").trim());\n    36\t}\n    37\t\n    38\tfunction parseAlignments(delimiterLine: string): Alignment[] {\n    39\t  const cols = splitTableRow(delimiterLine);\n    40\t  return cols.map((col) => {\n    41\t    const starts = col.startsWith(\":\");\n    42\t    const ends = col.endsWith(\":\");\n    43\t    if (starts && ends) return \"center\";\n    44\t    if (ends) return \"right\";\n    45\t    if (starts) return \"left\";\n    46\t    return null;\n    47\t  });\n    48\t}\n    49\t\n    50\tinterface MarkdownContentProps {\n    51\t  content: string;\n    52\t  className?: string;\n    53\t  inheritTextColor?: boolean;\n    54\t  projectRoot?: string | null;\n    55\t  onOpenProjectFile?: (file: ProjectFileReference) => void;\n    56\t}\n    57\t\n    58\tfunction linkClassName(inheritTextColor: boolean) {\n    59\t  return cn(\n    60\t    \"font-medium underline underline-offset-4 transition-colors\",\n    61\t    inheritTextColor\n    62\t      ? \"text-current decoration-current/35 hover:text-current dark:text-current dark:hover:text-current\"\n    63\t      : \"text-emerald-700 decoration-emerald-700/30 hover:text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:hover:text-emerald-100\",\n    64\t  );\n    65\t}\n    66\t\n    67\tfunction renderLink({\n    68\t  href,\n    69\t  label,\n    70\t  keyValue,\n    71\t  inheritTextColor,\n    72\t  projectRoot,\n    73\t  onOpenProjectFile,\n    74\t}: {\n    75\t  href: string;\n    76\t  label: string;\n    77\t  keyValue: string;\n    78\t  inheritTextColor: boolean;\n    79\t  projectRoot?: string | null;\n    80\t  onOpenProjectFile?: (file: ProjectFileReference) => void;\n    81\t}) {\n    82\t  const reference = projectRoot ? parseProjectFileReference(href, projectRoot) : null;\n    83\t  if (reference && onOpenProjectFile) {\n    84\t    return (\n    85\t      <ProjectFileContextMenu\n    86\t        key={keyValue}\n    87\t        reference={reference}\n    88\t        onOpen={onOpenProjectFile}\n    89\t      >\n    90\t        <button\n    91\t          type=\"button\"\n    92\t          className={cn(\"inline text-left\", linkClassName(inheritTextColor))}\n    93\t          onClick={() => onOpenProjectFile(reference)}\n    94\t        >\n    95\t          {label}\n    96\t        </button>\n    97\t      </ProjectFileContextMenu>\n    98\t    );\n    99\t  }\n   100\t\n   101\t  return isSafeHref(href) ? (\n   102\t    <a\n   103\t      key={keyValue}\n   104\t      href={href}\n   105\t      target={href.startsWith(\"http\") ? \"_blank\" : undefined}\n   106\t      rel={href.startsWith(\"http\") ? \"noreferrer\" : undefined}\n   107\t      className={linkClassName(inheritTextColor)}\n   108\t    >\n   109\t      {label}\n   110\t    </a>\n   111\t  ) : label;\n   112\t}\n   113\t\n   114\tfunction renderCodeSpan({\n   115\t  text,\n   116\t  keyValue,\n   117\t  inheritTextColor,\n   118\t  projectRoot,\n   119\t  onOpenProjectFile,\n   120\t}: {\n   121\t  text: string;\n   122\t  keyValue: string;\n   123\t  inheritTextColor: boolean;\n   124\t  projectRoot?: string | null;\n   125\t  onOpenProjectFile?: (file: ProjectFileReference) => void;\n   126\t}) {\n   127\t  const className = cn(\n   128\t    \"rounded bg-muted px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[0.85em]\",\n   129\t    inheritTextColor ? \"text-current\" : \"text-foreground\",\n   130\t  );\n   131\t  const reference = projectRoot ? parseProjectFileReference(text, projectRoot) : null;\n   132\t\n   133\t  if (reference && onOpenProjectFile) {\n   134\t    return (\n   135\t      <ProjectFileContextMenu\n   136\t        key={keyValue}\n   137\t        reference={reference}\n   138\t        onOpen={onOpenProjectFile}\n   139\t      >\n   140\t        <button\n   141\t          type=\"button\"\n   142\t          className={cn(\n   143\t            \"inline text-left underline decoration-current/35 underline-offset-4 transition-colors hover:decoration-current\",\n   144\t            className,\n   145\t          )}\n   146\t          onClick={() => onOpenProjectFile(reference)}\n   147\t        >\n   148\t          {text}\n   149\t        </button>\n   150\t      </ProjectFileContextMenu>\n   151\t    );\n   152\t  }\n   153\t\n   154\t  return (\n   155\t    <code\n   156\t      key={keyValue}\n   157\t      className={className}\n   158\t    >\n   159\t      {text}\n   160\t    </code>\n   161\t  );\n   162\t}\n   163\t\n   164\tfunction renderInlineMarkdown(\n   165\t  text: string,\n   166\t  keyPrefix: string,\n   167\t  inheritTextColor = false,\n   168\t  projectRoot?: string | null,\n   169\t  onOpenProjectFile?: (file: ProjectFileReference) => void,\n   170\t): React.ReactNode[] {\n   171\t  const nodes: React.ReactNode[] = [];\n   172\t  const pattern = /(\\[([^\\]]+)\\]\\(([^)\\s]+)\\)|`([^`]+)`|\\*\\*([^*]+)\\*\\*|\\*([^*]+)\\*|(https?:\\/\\/[^\\s<>()]+))/g;\n   173\t  let lastIndex = 0;\n   174\t  let match: RegExpExecArray | null;\n   175\t\n   176\t  while ((match = pattern.exec(text))) {\n   177\t    if (match.index > lastIndex) {\n   178\t      nodes.push(text.slice(lastIndex, match.index));\n   179\t    }\n   180\t\n   181\t    if (match[2] && match[3]) {\n   182\t      const href = match[3];\n   183\t      nodes.push(renderLink({\n   184\t        href,\n   185\t        label: match[2],\n   186\t        keyValue: `${keyPrefix}-link-${match.index}`,\n   187\t        inheritTextColor,\n   188\t        projectRoot,\n   189\t        onOpenProjectFile,\n   190\t      }));\n   191\t    } else if (match[4]) {\n   192\t      nodes.push(renderCodeSpan({\n   193\t        text: match[4],\n   194\t        keyValue: `${keyPrefix}-code-${match.index}`,\n   195\t        inheritTextColor,\n   196\t        projectRoot,\n   197\t        onOpenProjectFile,\n   198\t      }));\n   199\t    } else if (match[5]) {\n   200\t      nodes.push(\n   201\t        <strong\n   202\t          key={`${keyPrefix}-strong-${match.index}`}\n   203\t          className={cn(\"font-semibold\", inheritTextColor ? \"text-current\" : \"text-foreground\")}\n   204\t        >\n   205\t          {renderInlineMarkdown(\n   206\t            match[5],\n   207\t            `${keyPrefix}-strong-${match.index}`,\n   208\t            inheritTextColor,\n   209\t            projectRoot,\n   210\t            onOpenProjectFile,\n   211\t          )}\n   212\t        </strong>,\n   213\t      );\n   214\t    } else if (match[6]) {\n   215\t      nodes.push(<em key={`${keyPrefix}-em-${match.index}`}     "text": "     1\t# Client migration and platform limits\n     2\t\n     3\t## Shared behavior\n     4\t\n     5\tEvery client saves multiple runner profiles and keeps one main event connection\n     6\tfor every authenticated profile while its process is active. One workspace is\n     7\tvisible at a time. Inactive runner streams still update bounded previews and\n     8\tcan produce runner-labelled notifications.\n     9\t\n    10\tProfiles retain runner-scoped cursors, drafts, preferences, and bounded caches.\n    11\tAfter restart, clients bootstrap an authoritative snapshot, replay from the\n    12\tstored epoch-aware cursor when possible, and request a full resync when the\n    13\trunner reports epoch mismatch, cursor eviction, or subscriber overflow.\n    14\t\n    15\t## Electron migration\n    16\t\n    17\tThe desktop app no longer owns or starts a runner. On upgrade it imports the\n    18\tversioned legacy preferences/drafts export transactionally and creates an\n    19\texplicit former-local profile at `http://127.0.0.1:3050`. The user must start\n    20\tthat runner separately and log in with its shared password.\n    21\t\n    22\tProfile import writes a new version only after validation. A parse/write\n    23\tfailure restores the previous profile document and keeps the export for retry.\n    24\tOld runner data is not deleted automatically.\n    25\t\n    26\tElectron keeps profiles and pins in main-process files and sessions in\n    27\t`safeStorage`. If OS encryption is unavailable, new sessions are memory-only\n    28\tand the app explains that login will be required after restart.\n    29\t\n    30\t## VS Code migration\n    31\t\n    32\tThe extension migrates the old plain `omniHarness.sessionCookie` setting into\n    33\t`SecretStorage`, clears the plain setting, and marks the profile for\n    34\tre-authentication. A cookie is never reinterpreted as a bearer token. Profiles\n    35\tremain in `globalState`; the webview never receives session material.\n    36\t\n    37\t## Mobile build and runtime limits\n    38\t\n    39\tSee [the mobile README](../../apps/mobile/README.md) for toolchains and build\n    40\tcommands. Native source projects are committed; SDK caches, local signing\n    41\tstate, build products, provisioning profiles, keystores, and archives are not.\n    42\t\n    43\tiOS and Android keep all runner sockets open only while the app is active.\n    44\tWhen the OS suspends the app, native code persists each cursor and closes the\n    45\tstreams. Foreground resume reconnects and replays/resyncs. Version 1 does not\n    46\tpromise background sockets, background agent execution, or remote push after\n    47\tthe app has been suspended or terminated. Local notifications are best effort\n    48\twhile the host is active and permission is granted.\n    49\t\n    50\t## PWA offline behavior\n    51\t\n    52\tThe PWA caches the static shell and hashed assets only. It does not cache API\n    53\tresponses as authoritative runner state, queue mutations for later delivery,\n    54\tor claim that a conversation is current while offline. Offline startup can\n    55\tshow the shell and saved profile state; using a runner requires reconnect,\n    56\tbootstrap, and snapshot authority.\n    57\t\n    58\tBrowser storage is less isolated than an OS secret store. Use the PWA only on a\n    59\ttrusted browser profile and treat script/browser-extension compromise as access\n    60\tto its bearer sessions.\n    61\t\n    62\t## Version 1 non-goals\n    63\t\n    64\t- merged dashboards or search across runner data;\n    65\t- runner-to-runner handoff;\n    66\t- background mobile push delivery;\n    67\t- silent identity replacement or automatic certificate trust;\n    68\t- a runner embedded in Electron, iOS, Android, or VS Code;\n    69\t- extracting the repository into separate packages;\n    70\t- offline mutation queues or preview caches that substitute for a snapshot.\n    71\t\n    72\tThe runner still manages a co-located API process and ACP bridge process as one\n    73\tlogical deployment. Platform clients speak only to the API server.\n     1\t# Common Runtime Multi-Surface Architecture\n     2\t\n     3\t## Intent\n     4\t\n     5\tOmniHarness should behave like one local supervision runtime with many thin\n     6\tinterfaces. The browser, standalone server, native shells, editor extensions,\n     7\tand future surfaces should share runtime services and renderer contracts rather\n     8\tthan duplicating conversation, worker, event, settings, filesystem, or auth\n     9\tbehavior.\n    10\t\n    11\tThis follows the useful OpenChamber pattern:\n    12\t\n    13\t- a shared runtime/server owns product behavior,\n    14\t- a shared React renderer owns UI behavior,\n    15\t- each shell supplies transport and native capabilities only.\n    16\t\n    17\t## Implemented architecture\n    18\t\n    19\tThe split now includes:\n    20\t\n    21\t- `src/runtime/index.ts`: runtime lifecycle handle with named events for start,\n    22\t  stop, and start failure.\n    23\t- `src/runtime/bootstrap.ts`: portable bootstrap builder shared by the Next page\n    24\t  and `/api/runtime/bootstrap`.\n    25\t- `src/runtime/http/registry.ts`: portable Fetch-style route registry.\n    26\t- `src/runtime/http/context.ts`: common request context builder.\n    27\t- `src/runtime/http/adapters/next.ts`: Next route adapter for portable handlers.\n    28\t- `src/runtime/http/server.ts`: local Node HTTP server plus\n    29\t  `startOmniServer(...)` runtime-backed handle. It can also serve staged\n    30\t  renderer assets for shell packaging.\n    31\t- `src/runtime-api/types.ts`: transport-neutral `RuntimeAPIs` contract covering\n    32\t  auth, runs, conversations, workers, files, git, planning, settings,\n    33\t  accounts, notifications, terminals, bootstrap, and events.\n    34\t- `src/runtime-api/web.ts`: browser adapter over HTTP and EventSource.\n    35\t- `src/runtime-api/electron.ts`: validated Electron IPC adapter; runner\n    36\t  networking and bearer credentials remain in the main process.\n    37\t- `src/runtime-api/capacitor.ts`: validated iOS/Android plugin adapter; runner\n    38\t  networking, SSE, TLS pins, and credentials remain native.\n    39\t- `src/runtime-api/vscode.ts`: VS Code webview adapter over `postMessage`.\n    40\t- `src/runtime-api/provider.tsx`: React provider for injecting runtime APIs.\n    41\t- `src/ui/OmniApp.tsx`: renderer root wrapping the existing Home app.\n    42\t- `src/ui/render-web.tsx`: embeddable React renderer entry used by packaged\n    43\t  shell builds.\n    44\t- `apps/electron`: pure remote client. It serves packaged assets from\n    45\t  `app://omniharness`, blocks renderer networking, and owns all runner\n    46\t  HTTP/SSE, credential, and TLS operations.\n    47\t- `apps/mobile`: Capacitor iOS/Android clients with URLSession/OkHttp streams,\n    48\t  Keychain/Android-Keystore sessions, per-profile pinning, lifecycle cursor\n    49\t  recovery, links, and local notifications.\n    50\t- `apps/vscode`: remote extension with profile-scoped HTTP/SSE in the extension\n    51\t  host, `SecretStorage` sessions, and editor actions.\n    52\t- `src/runtime/http/routes`: migrated portable route handlers for bootstrap,\n    53\t  auth/session/login/logout/pairing, settings, accounts, agents, model\n    54\t  discovery, notifications, plans, project memory, planning review/promote,\n    55\t  attachments, filesystem, git, conversations, messages, queued messages,\n    56\t  worker entries, supervisor, run lifecycle mutations, run answer/resume,\n    57\t  event-log diagnostics, and event snapshot/SSE streaming,\n    58\t  mounted through the shared runtime route registry and used by the existing\n    59\t  Next route files as thin adapters.\n    60\t\n    61\t## Surface Responsibilities\n    62\t\n    63\tWeb/PWA:\n    64\t\n    65\t- loads the Vite static interface served by a runner,\n    66\t- receives escaped request bootstrap JSON injected into the HTML template,\n    67\t- uses same-origin cookies for the serving runner and PKCE-approved bearer\n    68\t  sessions for additional runners.\n    69\t\n    70\tStandalone runtime server:\n    71\t\n    72\t- starts an `OmniRuntime`,\n    73\t- serves registered portable HTTP handlers,\n    74\t- owns clean shutdown through the returned server handle.\n    75\t\n    76\tVS Code:\n    77\t\n    78\t- stores multiple runner profiles in `globalState`,\n    79\t- owns profile-scoped HTTP and SSE in the extension host,\n    80\t- keeps bearer material in `SecretStorage`,\n    81\t- exposes editor capabilities through typed bridge messages.\n    82\t\n    83\tElectron:\n    84\t\n    85\t- never imports or starts runner code,\n    86\t- loads the shared renderer from `app://omniharness`,\n    87\t- blocks renderer HTTP/SSE and injects sessions only in main-process requests,\n    88\t- stores credentials with `safeStorage` and pins per profile/origin.\n    89\t\n    90\tiOS/Android:\n    91\t\n    92\t- load the same `dist/interface` artifact through Capacitor,\n    93\t- own URLSession/OkHttp requests and streams in native plugins,\n    94\t- store bearer sessions in Keychain/Android Keystore,\n    95\t- persist cursors on suspension and reconnect/resync on resume.\n    96\t\n    97\tAll profile-aware clients keep one main stream per authenticated runner while\n    98\ttheir process is active. Only the active runner owns visible terminal streams.\n    99\t\n   100\t## Route Inventory And Migration Order\n   101\t\n   102\tCurrent route inventory:\n   103\t\n   104\t| Route | Runtime owner / dependencies | Portable status |\n   105\t| --- | --- | --- |\n   106\t| `/api/runtime/bootstrap` | `buildRuntimeBootstrap` | Migrated |\n   107\t| `/api/auth/session` | auth config/session/audit | Migrated |\n   108\t| `/api/auth/login` | auth config/password/session/audit/rate-limit | Migrated |\n   109\t| `/api/auth/logout` | auth guards/session/audit | Migrated |\n   110\t| `/api/auth/pair` | auth config/guards/pairing/audit | Migrated |\n   111\t| `/api/auth/pair/redeem` | auth config/pairing/session/audit | Migrated |\n   112\t| `/api/settings` | settings table, crypto, project root canonicalization | Migrated |\n   113\t| `/api/accounts` | accounts table | Migrated |\n   114\t| `/api/agents` | bridge client/runtime worker snapshots | Migrated |\n   115\t| `/api/agents/catalog` | agent catalog/configuration diagnostics | Migrated |\n   116\t| `/api/agents/[name]` | bridge client/runtime worker detail/history | Migrated |\n   117\t| `/api/llm-models` | model discovery | Migrated |\n   118\t| `/api/codex-auth/status` | Codex auth reader | Migrated |\n   119\t| `/api/notifications` | auth guards, notification preferences, web push | Migrated |\n   120\t| `/api/projects/memory` | auth guards, memory tools, project settings, runs lookup | Migrated |\n     1\t# Runner deployment and operations\n     2\t\n     3\t## Process model\n     4\t\n     5\tOne OmniHarness runner is one logical deployment with two co-located\n     6\tprocesses:\n     7\t\n     8\t- the API server owns SQLite, auth, supervision, PTYs, settings, files, and the\n     9\t  HTTP/SSE contract;\n    10\t- the ACP bridge owns agent processes and listens on loopback by default.\n    11\t\n    12\tClients connect only to the API server. Electron, iOS, Android, VS Code, and\n    13\tbrowser/PWA clients never connect directly to the bridge.\n    14\t\n    15\t`pnpm runner` starts the API server and starts or adopts a loopback bridge. The\n    16\tAPI remains available in a degraded state while a failed bridge retries. All\n    17\tstart, adopt, retry, refusal, failure, and shutdown choices emit named events.\n    18\t\n    19\t## Build and start\n    20\t\n    21\tBuild the shared static interface and start a normal runner:\n    22\t\n    23\t```bash\n    24\tpnpm install\n    25\tpnpm build\n    26\tpnpm runner\n    27\t```\n    28\t\n    29\tThe default API/UI address is `http://0.0.0.0:3050`; the managed bridge defaults\n    30\tto `http://127.0.0.1:7800`.\n    31\t\n    32\tPort `3050` is also the existing public-tunnel origin contract. Replacing the\n    33\tweb framework or static-interface implementation does not authorize changing\n    34\tthat deployed port or the configured public hostname. During a cutover, start\n    35\tthe replacement runner on the existing origin first and verify the public\n    36\thealth endpoint before changing any tunnel route.\n    37\t\n    38\tSupported runner options:\n    39\t\n    40\t```text\n    41\t--host <address>\n    42\t--port <0-65535>\n    43\t--bridge-url <url>\n    44\t--static-dir <directory>\n    45\t--no-static\n    46\t```\n    47\t\n    48\tThe equivalent environment settings are:\n    49\t\n    50\t```text\n    51\tOMNIHARNESS_RUNNER_HOST\n    52\tOMNIHARNESS_RUNNER_PORT (PORT is a compatibility fallback)\n    53\tOMNIHARNESS_BRIDGE_URL\n    54\tOMNIHARNESS_STATIC_DIR\n    55\tOMNIHARNESS_NO_STATIC=1\n    56\tOMNIHARNESS_MANAGE_BRIDGE=0\n    57\t```\n    58\t\n    59\tWith the default build, the runner serves `dist/interface`. `--no-static` is a\n    60\tsupported API-only mode. If the default artifact is absent, startup continues\n    61\tand emits `runner.static_ui_missing`; if an explicitly supplied `--static-dir`\n    62\tis missing or invalid, startup fails. Never combine `--no-static` and\n    63\t`--static-dir`.\n    64\t\n    65\t## Instances, data, and locks\n    66\t\n    67\t`OMNIHARNESS_ROOT` selects the base data root. `OMNIHARNESS_INSTANCE=name`\n    68\tplaces one isolated deployment under\n    69\t`$OMNIHARNESS_ROOT/instances/name`. Each instance owns:\n    70\t\n    71\t```text\n    72\tsqlite.db\n    73\t.omniharness-auth.key\n    74\trunner.lock.json\n    75\tbridge.lock.json\n    76\tapp-data/run-data/\n    77\t```\n    78\t\n    79\tThe SQLite database and worker JSONL files are one consistency domain. Do not\n    80\tpoint two live runners at the same instance root. A second process refuses an\n    81\towned runner lock. Separate instances need separate API ports and bridge URLs.\n    82\t\n    83\t## TLS and reverse proxies\n    84\t\n    85\tThe Node runner currently terminates HTTP. For any non-loopback deployment,\n    86\tterminate HTTPS at a reverse proxy or private tunnel and expose only the API/UI\n    87\tport. Keep the ACP bridge on loopback.\n    88\t\n    89\tForwarding headers are ignored unless the socket peer matches an explicit\n    90\tcomma-separated `OMNIHARNESS_TRUSTED_PROXIES` rule. Rules may be exact IPs or\n    91\tIPv4 CIDRs:\n    92\t\n    93\t```text\n    94\tOMNIHARNESS_TRUSTED_PROXIES=127.0.0.1,10.20.0.0/16\n    95\tOMNIHARNESS_PUBLIC_ORIGIN=https://runner.example.net\n    96\t```\n    97\t\n    98\tConfigure the proxy to replace, not append untrusted client values for,\n    99\t`Forwarded`/`X-Forwarded-*`. Never use a catch-all trusted-proxy range.\n   100\t\n   101\t## Backup and restore\n   102\t\n   103\tStop the runner before a filesystem backup. Copy the entire instance directory,\n   104\tincluding the database, auth key, worker streams, locks, settings, and managed\n   105\tmetadata. Exclude only transient build caches. Also back up the deployment's\n   106\tpassword-hash configuration separately.\n   107\t\n   108\tRestore into an empty instance path while the runner is stopped, preserve file\n   109\tpermissions, then start it with the same `OMNIHARNESS_ROOT` and\n   110\t`OMNIHARNESS_INSTANCE`. The auth key is required to read encrypted settings and\n   111\tvalidate existing sessions. If it is intentionally omitted, rotate the\n   112\tpassword and expect every client to authenticate again.\n   113\t\n   114\tClient profile lists and platform-secure bearer sessions are client state, not\n   115\tpart of a runner backup.\n   116\t\n   117\t## Disposable smoke check\n   118\t\n   119\tThis uses an isolated root and a hidden prompt. The password is not passed as a\n   120\tcommand-line argument or printed:\n   121\t\n   122\t```bash\n   123\tsmoke_root=\"$(mktemp -d \"${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/omniharness-smoke.XXXXXX\")\"\n   124\ttrap 'test -n \"$smoke_root\" && rm -rf -- \"$smoke_root\"' EXIT\n   125\tread -r -s -p \"Temporary runner password: \" smoke_password\n   126\tprintf '\\n'\n   127\texport OMNIHARNESS_AUTH_PASSWORD=\"$smoke_password\"\n   128\tunset smoke_password\n   129\tOMNIHARNESS_ROOT=\"$smoke_root\" \\\n   130\tOMNIHARNESS_INSTANCE=smoke \\\n   131\tpnpm runner --host 127.0.0.1 --port 3059 --no-static\n   132\t```\n   133\t\n   134\tIn another terminal, check liveness with:\n   135\t\n   136\t```bash\n   137\tcurl --fail --silent --show-error http://127.0.0.1:3059/api/healthz\n   138\t```\n   139\t\n   140\tPress Ctrl-C in the runner terminal. The trap removes only the temporary root\n\nPRODUCT\n     1\t# Product\n     2\t\n     3\t## Register\n     4\t\n     5\tproduct\n     6\t\n     7\t## Users\n     8\t\n     9\tSoftware developers and technical leads supervising several coding agents across local, remote, desktop, editor, and mobile surfaces. They work in long-running implementation and debugging sessions where clear state, rapid switching, durable recovery, and trustworthy control matter more than decorative presentation.\n    10\t\n    11\t## Product Purpose\n    12\t\n    13\tOmniHarness is a durable control plane for starting, observing, steering, and recovering ACP-backed coding-agent work. Success means a user can understand what every runner and worker is doing, take the next safe action quickly, move between devices and servers without losing context, and trust that failures, credentials, and recovery states are represented honestly.\n    14\t\n    15\t## Brand Personality\n    16\t\n    17\tCalm, capable, direct. The interface should feel like a precise professional tool with a human voice: dense when the work demands it, quiet when it does not, and explicit about uncertainty or failure.\n    18\t\n    19\t## Anti-references\n    20\t\n    21\t- Generic analytics dashboards built from interchangeable metric cards.\n    22\t- Sci-fi command centers with neon decoration, fake telemetry, or gratuitous motion.\n    23\t- Chat interfaces that hide execution state and recovery controls behind conversational chrome.\n    24\t- Consumer onboarding that stretches a simple connection task into a promotional sequence.\n    25\t- Interfaces that make remote, offline, authentication, or identity failures look like temporary loading.\n    26\t\n    27\t## Design Principles\n    28\t\n    29\t1. Show authority and scope. Every action, status, notification, and cache belongs visibly to one runner and one conversation.\n    30\t2. Keep supervision fast. Frequent actions remain close to the active work, with familiar controls and strong keyboard behavior.\n    31\t3. Make failure honest and recoverable. Name the affected runner, explain the state, and offer the smallest safe next action.\n    32\t4. Preserve context across time and surfaces. Switching, reconnecting, and returning later should not erase drafts, cursors, or the user’s mental model.\n    33\t5. Let the work dominate. Navigation and status remain legible but visually restrained around agent output, terminal content, and decisions.\n    34\t\n    35\t## Accessibility & Inclusion\n    36\t\n    37\tTarget WCAG 2.2 AA. All flows must support keyboard navigation, visible focus, screen readers, zoom and narrow layouts, live language changes, reduced motion, and status communication that does not rely on color alone.\n"
  }

lines.slice(-MAX_OUTPUT_LINES).join(\"\") : lines.join(\"\");\n    22\t  let truncated = lines.length > MAX_OUTPUT_LINES;\n    23\t  const bytes = Buffer.from(next, \"utf8\");\n    24\t  if (bytes.length > byteLimit) {\n    25\t    let start = bytes.length - byteLimit;\n    26\t    while (start < bytes.length && (bytes[start]! & 0xc0) === 0x80) start += 1;\n    27\t    next = bytes.subarray(start).toString(\"utf8\");\n    28\t    truncated = true;\n    29\t  }\n    30\t  return { output: next, truncated };\n    31\t}\n    32\t\n    33\texport class TerminalService {\n    34\t  private readonly terminals = new Map<string, TerminalRecord>();\n    35\t\n    36\t  constructor(\n    37\t    private readonly onSnapshot?: (terminalId: string, snapshot: acp.TerminalOutputResponse) => void,\n    38\t    private readonly onLifecycle?: (action: \"created\" | \"released\", terminalId: string) => void,\n    39\t  ) {}\n    40\t\n    41\t  get size() {\n    42\t    return this.terminals.size;\n    43\t  }\n    44\t\n    45\t  private get(sessionId: string, terminalId: string) {\n    46\t    const terminal = this.terminals.get(terminalId);\n    47\t    if (!terminal || terminal.sessionId !== sessionId) {\n    48\t      throw acp.RequestError.invalidParams({ terminalId }, \"Unknown terminal\");\n    49\t    }\n    50\t    return terminal;\n    51\t  }\n    52\t\n    53\t  async create(params: acp.CreateTerminalRequest): Promise<acp.CreateTerminalResponse> {\n    54\t    const terminalId = randomUUID();\n    55\t    const outputByteLimit = Math.max(1, Math.min(\n    56\t      MAX_OUTPUT_BYTE_LIMIT,\n    57\t      params.outputByteLimit ?? DEFAULT_OUTPUT_BYTE_LIMIT,\n    58\t    ));\n    59\t    const env = params.env?.reduce<NodeJS.ProcessEnv>((result, item) => {\n    60\t      result[item.name] = item.value;\n    61\t      return result;\n    62\t    }, { ...process.env });\n    63\t    const child = spawn(params.command, params.args ?? [], {\n    64\t      cwd: params.cwd ?? undefined,\n    65\t      env,\n    66\t      stdio: [\"pipe\", \"pipe\", \"pipe\"],\n    67\t    }) as ChildProcessWithoutNullStreams;\n    68\t    let resolveExit!: (status: acp.TerminalExitStatus) => void;\n    69\t    const exited = new Promise<acp.TerminalExitStatus>((resolve) => {\n    70\t      resolveExit = resolve;\n    71\t    });\n    72\t    const record: TerminalRecord = {\n    73\t      sessionId: params.sessionId,\n    74\t      child,\n    75\t      output: \"\",\n    76\t      truncated: false,\n    77\t      outputByteLimit,\n    78\t      exitStatus: null,\n    79\t      exited,\n    80\t    };\n    81\t    const append = (chunk: Buffer) => {\n    82\t      const bounded = boundedOutput(record.output, chunk.toString(\"utf8\"), record.outputByteLimit);\n    83\t      record.output = bounded.output;\n    84\t      record.truncated ||= bounded.truncated;\n    85\t    };\n    86\t    child.stdout.on(\"data\", append);\n    87\t    child.stderr.on(\"data\", append);\n    88\t    child.once(\"close\", (exitCode, signal) => {\n    89\t      record.exitStatus = { exitCode, signal };\n    90\t      resolveExit(record.exitStatus);\n    91\t      this.onSnapshot?.(terminalId, { output: record.output, truncated: record.truncated, exitStatus: record.exitStatus });\n    92\t    });\n    93\t    child.once(\"error\", () => {\n    94\t      if (!record.exitStatus) {\n    95\t        record.exitStatus = { exitCode: null, signal: null };\n    96\t        resolveExit(record.exitStatus);\n    97\t      }\n    98\t    });\n    99\t    this.terminals.set(terminalId, record);\n   100\t    this.onLifecycle?.(\"created\", terminalId);\n   101\t    return { terminalId };\n   102\t  }\n   103\t\n   104\t  async output(params: acp.TerminalOutputRequest): Promise<acp.TerminalOutputResponse> {\n   105\t    const terminal = this.get(params.sessionId, params.terminalId);\n   106\t    const response = {\n   107\t      output: terminal.output,\n   108\t      truncated: terminal.truncated,\n   109\t      ...(terminal.exitStatus ? { exitStatus: terminal.exitStatus } : {}),\n   110\t    };\n   111\t    this.onSnapshot?.(params.terminalId, response);\n   112\t    return response;\n   113\t  }\n   114\t\n   115\t  async wait(params: acp.WaitForTerminalExitRequest): Promise<acp.WaitForTerminalExitResponse> {\n   116\t    return this.get(params.sessionId, params.terminalId).exited;\n   117\t  }\n   118\t\n   119\t  async kill(params: acp.KillTerminalRequest): Promise<acp.KillTerminalResponse> {\n   120\t    const terminal = this.get(params.sessionId, params.terminalId);\n   121\t    if (!terminal.exitStatus) terminal.child.kill(\"SIGTERM\");\n   122\t    return {};\n   123\t  }\n   124\t\n   125\t  async release(params: acp.ReleaseTerminalRequest): Promise<acp.ReleaseTerminalResponse> {\n   126\t    const terminal = this.get(params.sessionId, params.terminalId);\n   127\t    if (!terminal.exitStatus) terminal.child.kill(\"SIGTERM\");\n   128\t    this.terminals.delete(params.terminalId);\n   129\t    this.onLifecycle?.(\"released\", params.terminalId);\n   130\t    return {};\n   131\t  }\n   132\t\n   133\t  dispose() {\n   134\t    for (const [terminalId, terminal] of this.terminals) {\n   135\t      if (!terminal.exitStatus) terminal.child.kill(\"SIGTERM\");\n   136\t      this.onLifecycle?.(\"released\", terminalId);\n   137\t    }\n   138\t    this.terminals.clear();\n   139\t  }\n   140\t}\n\nWORKER TERMINAL PROCESS 47\tfunction quoteCommandPart(value: string): string {\n    48\t  if (/^[A-Za-z0-9_./:=@%+-]+$/.test(value)) {\n    49\t    return value;\n    50\t  }\n    51\t\n    52\t  return `'${value.replace(/'/g, \"'\\\\''\")}'`;\n    53\t}\n    54\t\n    55\tfunction asCommandString(value: unknown): string | null {\n    56\t  const direct = asNonEmptyString(value);\n    57\t  if (direct) {\n    58\t    return direct;\n    59\t  }\n    60\t\n    61\t  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {\n    62\t    return null;\n    63\t  }\n    64\t\n    65\t  const parts = value\n    66\t    .map((part) => typeof part === \"string\" ? part.trim() : \"\")\n    67\t    .filter(Boolean);\n    68\t\n    69\t  return parts.length > 0 ? parts.map(quoteCommandPart).join(\" \") : null;\n    70\t}\n    71\t\n    72\tfunction normalizeMultilineText(value: string): string {\n    73\t  return value.replace(/\\r\\n?/g, \"\\n\").trim();\n    74\t}\n    75\t\n    76\tfunction normalizeStatus(value: string | null | undefined): WorkerTerminalProcessStatus {\n    77\t  const normalized = value?.trim().toLowerCase();\n    78\t  switch (normalized) {\n    79\t    case \"pending\":\n    80\t    case \"in_progress\":\n    81\t    case \"running\":\n    82\t    case \"working\":\n    83\t    case \"completed\":\n    84\t    case \"failed\":\n    85\t    case \"error\":\n    86\t    case \"cancelled\":\n    87\t      return normalized;\n    88\t    case \"canceled\":\n    89\t      return \"cancelled\";\n    90\t    case \"done\":\n    91\t    case \"success\":\n    92\t      return \"completed\";\n    93\t    case undefined:\n    94\t    case \"\":\n    95\t      return \"unknown\";\n    96\t    default:\n    97\t      return \"unknown\";\n (value == null) {\n   106\t    return null;\n   107\t  }\n   108\t  try {\n   109\t    return JSON.stringify(value, null, 2);\n   110\t  } catch {\n   111\t    return String(value);\n   112\t  }\n value\n   121\t    .map((item) => {\n   122\t      const record = asRecord(item);\n   123\t      if (!record) {\n   124\t        return null;\n   125\t      }\n   126\t\n   127\t      const nested = parts.join(\"\\n\\n\") : null;\n   137\t}\n   138\t\n   139\tfunction extractCommand(raw: Record<string, unknown> | null): string | null {\n   140\t  if (!raw) {\n   141\t    return null;\n   142\t     "text": "     1\t# Managed Binary Trust Must Be Rechecked at Launch\n     2\t\n     3\t**Date:** 2026-07-13\n     4\t**Context:** OmniHarness managed CLIProxyAPI integration\n     5\t**Symptom:** The installer verified the downloaded archive, but later service startup could trust a pre-existing binary and `current.json` without re-hashing the executable. Archive links were also initially checked only after extraction.\n     6\t**Root Cause:** Verification was treated as an installation-time concern instead of a continuing execution boundary. Metadata ownership, binary integrity, archive entry type, and process identity were checked by separate paths with inconsistent strength.\n     7\t**Fix:** Tar link and special-file entries are rejected before extraction. Managed versions are assembled in a temporary directory and atomically renamed with an owned SHA-256 record. Both installation reuse and every service inspection/start re-hash the binary. Process ownership now requires exact argv and OS start-time matching, and child cleanup covers every post-spawn failure.\n     8\t**Verification:** Installer tests reject link entries and unowned pre-existing binaries, detect a tampered installed executable, and preserve atomic version ownership. Process tests reject reused PIDs and lookalike config arguments and clean up when ownership recording fails.\n     9\t**Prevention:** Treat every execution of a downloaded binary as a fresh trust decision. Authenticate archives before extraction, install atomically, re-check owned metadata and content hashes at launch, and never signal a PID without exact command and start-time Even benign upstream releases can silently move the installed adapters beyond OmniHarness’s tested contract.\n- **Effort**: M\n- **Risk**: MED — release publishing and upgrade workflows must remain usable.\n- **Confidence**: HIGH\n- **Fix sketch**: Publish immutable versions and checksums, install atomically, store owned version/hash metadata, re-hash at launch, and pin Claude/Cargo installs to tested versions or commits.\n\n### [CORRECTNESS-01] Preserve screenshots as bounded durable artifacts instead of truncating base64\n\n- **Evidence**: `src/server/agent-runtime/output-store.ts:10` — live raw strings are capped at 4,000 characters.\n- **Evidence**: `src/server/agent-runtime/output-store.ts:128` — raw content is truncated by string slicing before it reaches live records.\n- **Evidence**: `src/components/Terminal.tsx:1707` — image content is rendered by treating the stored raw string as complete base64.\n- **Evidence**: `tests/app/acp/protocol-activity.test.ts:11` — image coverage uses a one-byte payload, so it cannot detect realistic screenshot truncation.\n- **Evidence**: `src/server/artifacts/stream-types.ts:10` — artifact streams have no media/build-artifact kind.\n- **Impact**: Normal screenshots exceed 4 KB, so inline ACP images can become invalid while the worker is live. Add realistic image-size, reload, remote-client, cleanup, and quota tests.\n\n### [CORRECTNESS-02] Make agent-created screenshot files viewable from the conversation\n\n- **Evidence**: `src/server/fs/files.ts:109` — the project-file endpoint rejects binary files.\n- **Evidence**: `src/components/home/FileViewerPanel.tsx:156` — the viewer supports Markdown or syntax-highlighted text only.\n- **Evidence**: `src/components/MarkdownContent.tsx:172` — inline parsing handles links and text formatting but not Markdown image syntax.\n- **Evidence**: `src/components/Terminal.tsx:1707` — screenshots are visible only if an adapter emits an ACP image content block.\n- **Impact**: Codex or Claude can successfully save an emulator/browser screenshot in the project and report its path, but a remote OmniHarness user cannot preview it. Adapter drift can therefore remain invisible until a user hits the missing path.\n- **Effort**: M\n- **Risk**: MED — a truthful gate may initially disable or fail features currently claimed as operational.\n- **Confidence**: HIGH\n- **Fix sketch**: Derive exhaustive discriminators from the installed schema, bind each registry item to behavior tests, compare the live adapter handshake/feature matrix, and reject or disable unverified capabilities.\n\n### [ARCHITECTURE-01] Give emulator and GUI processes complete lifecycle ownership\n\n- **Evidence**: `src/server/agent-runtime/acp/terminal-service.ts:36` — the terminal lifecycle callback models only `created` and `released`.\n- **Evidence**: `src/server/agent-runtime/acp/terminal-service.ts:88` — process exit records a snapshot but emits no typed exited lifecycle event.\n- **Evidence**: `src/server/agent-runtime/acp/terminal-service.ts:119` — kill sends `SIGTERM` without a killed event or process-tree ownership.\n- **Evidence**: `src/server/agent-runtime/acp/runtime-client.ts:302` — terminal events are projected only as generic resource created/released.\n- **Evidence**: `src/server/events/named-events.ts:616` — the event union has no ACP terminal exited/killed/failed states.\n- **Impact**: Long-running simulators, emulators, dev servers, and GUI automation sessions cannot be reliably distinguished as running, exited, killed, leaked, or recoverable. Restart and cleanup assertions cannot prove settlement.\n- **Effort**: L\n- **Risk**: HIGH — process-group handling and cross-platform shutdown semantics are delicate.\n- **Confidence**: HIGH for the observability gap; MED for actual child-process leakage.\n- **Fix sketch**: Model terminal/process resources with typed created/started/exited/killed/failed/released events, process-tree ownership, restart reconciliation, and lifecycle scenarios for detached or long-running tools.\n\n### [DIRECTION-01] Add capability-aware runner selection and artifact transfer\n\n- **Evidence**: `docs/platforms/client-migration-and-limits.md:62` — runner-to-runner handoff and embedded runners are explicit v1 non-goals.\n- **Evidence**: `docs/platforms/client-migration-and-limits.md:72` — platform clients talk only to a separately deployed runner.\n- **Evidence**: `docs/architecture/common-runtime-multi-surface.md:97` — clients keep multiple runner connections but expose one active runner’s workspace.\n- **Impact**: A mobile build must run wherever the selected runner lives.
